| **DECISION-261** | ICT/SMC PIT rules — Pass 52 owner-approved with N+1 lag rule. Pattern completion lag: ICT/SMC patterns complete on bar N (e.g., 3-bar FVG forms at N-2/N-1/N); actionable signal is bar N+1 open at earliest. Rationale: at end-of-day N, pattern is "known" but no further trading possible same day for daily bars; earliest tradable execution is bar N+1 open. For intra-bar events (BOS, CHoCH, liquidity grab): same N+1 rule — events recorded at bar N (close confirms event) but actionable from bar N+1 open. Joint with DEC-040 PIT loader + DEC-259 cache schema (lag computed at consumption time, not stored as offset in cache). **Use-case map (per #57):** ICT/SMC strategies (DEC-259 consumers), PIT loader uniform lag application, backtest correctness. ~0.5 days rule documentation + 2-line check in strategy entry logic. **Test signals:** synthetic FVG forms at bar 100 → strategy entry at bar 101 open (not bar 100); zero-lag entry triggers test failure flag. **Pass 52 turn 77 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED (bookkeeping flip per status drift): ICT/SMC PIT rules with N+1 lag rule. Pattern completion lag: ICT/SMC patterns complete on bar N; actionable signal is bar N+1 open at earliest. Pattern "known" at end-of-day N but no same-day trading for daily bars. For intra-bar events (BOS, CHoCH, liquidity grab): same N+1 rule. Sprint 1 work item per ENGINEERING_REGISTER. Joint with DEC-259 (ICT/SMC cache uses this rule). PIT correctness foundational — without N+1 lag, ICT signals would have lookahead bias. ~0.5d (documentation rule + enforcement test). Test signal: synthetic FVG forms at bar 100 → strategy entry triggers at bar 101 open (not bar 100 close).** | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Batch X41 — Phase 0.A Data Prefetch Gaps | Pass 47 | - |

Map endpoints → 60+ strategies, ~11 agents, ~17 cube dimensions; (6) Caching & rate-limit feasibility for S&P 500 × 5-year scope (per DEC-411).** Each meaningful gap becomes candidate sub-decision (DEC-442+). **OpenBB consumption gap (PROJECT_PLAN says ✓, code shows zero):** dual treatment per Pass 52 owner approval — flag in API_AUDIT.md OpenBB section + sub-decision to either consume or remove from project scope (NOT a BUG; it's unresolved scope per audit-as-source-of-truth principle). **Deliverable:** new file `API_AUDIT.md` committed to repo, organized in 4 tiers above. **Effort revised:** ~10-14 days (was ~5-6d for 7 APIs; ~10-14d for ~17 APIs with hypothesis-driven depth per Polygon analysis pattern). **Pre-condition gate before next backtest pass run.** Sub-decisions to be created per API after audit. | **DECISION-459** | AgentGateConfig spec — Option C Hybrid Architecture **Pass 52 turn 129 owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED (SUPERSEDES DEC-042):** OPTION C HYBRID ARCHITECTURE — TradingAgents Portfolio Manager native output as primary signal + separate Risk veto layer. Origin: DEC-042 turn 121 spec was executed without verifying that named agents (Bull/Bear/Risk/ChartAnalyst as parallel voters) match actual TradingAgents architecture. Owner accountability question turn 128 surfaced the gap. Re-resolution per owner directive turn 129. ARCHITECTURE: (1) PRIMARY SIGNAL — TradingAgents Portfolio Manager outputs structured Pydantic decision per propagate(): {decision: BUY/HOLD/SELL, confidence: 0.0-1.0, rationale, structured_fields...}. Confidence is consumed directly as primary gate signal (NOT re-aggregated from intermediate agent scores). (2) TIER ASSIGNMENT — confidence threshold maps to DEC-021 3-tier (HIGH ≥0.8 = 5%, MED 0.65-0.8 = 3%, LOW 0.5-0.65 = 1.5%); confidence < 0.5 → REJECT. (3) RISK VETO LAYER — separate Risk veto evaluated AFTER Portfolio Manager confidence threshold passed. Implementation options for Risk veto signal: (a) Extract Risk debate confidence from LangGraph state (preferred), (b) Run separate Risk Manager call on PM-approved candidates only (cost+latency overhead, simpler), (c) Use Portfolio Manager risk-adjusted confidence as combined signal (no separate veto layer — collapses to Option B Pure-PM-native). Default: extract from LangGraph state per (a). (4) RISK VETO LOGIC — `s_risk ≥ 0.5` hard gate; if breached → REJECT regardless of PM confidence. Owner directive #3 turn 121 carried forward: extensive A/B testing of continuous-Risk-score variants (continuous-Risk-weighted into PM confidence vs binary-veto) required REVISIT_AFTER_BACKTEST. (5) BULL/BEAR ALIGNMENT — PRESERVED from DEC-042 turn 121 spec but applied at debate-level: extract Research Manager synthesis from LangGraph state; if Research Manager indicates contested/no-consensus position (Bull-Bear debate didn't converge to high-confidence direction) → REJECT. Operationalization: Research Manager confidence ≥ 0.5 AND direction matches PM decision direction. (6) DECISION DIRECTION — uses PM decision field directly (BUY/HOLD/SELL); avoids parallel-voting interpretation issues from DEC-042. (7) OVERRIDE — Stage 2 backtest deterministic (no override); Stage 3+ owner manual override via dashboard (carried from DEC-042). A/B FRAMEWORK INTEGRATION (DEC-205-216 + DEC-211): (arm A) rules-only: bypass agents entirely; (arm B) full-agents-with-veto: PM confidence + Risk veto layer (default config); (arm C) no-Risk: Risk veto disabled (PM confidence only — collapses to Option B); (arm D) no-Bull-Bear-align: Research Manager alignment check disabled; (arm E ablation per DEC-211): per-phase weight variations on intermediate scores. JOINT WITH: DEC-021 (3-tier sizing — modifier consumer); DEC-042 (predecessor SUPERSEDED); DEC-051 (TradingAgents staged adoption — provides Portfolio Manager); DEC-058 (LLM provider strategy: GPT-5.4-mini Stage 2 backtest, Anthropic Stage 3+ live); DEC-062 (TradingAgents 5-tier → position_size_modifier — adapted: PM confidence replaces 5-tier as primary signal); DEC-131 (agent value-add ≥0.2 net Sharpe gate); DEC-205-216 (A/B cluster — arm definitions revised per Option C); DEC-211 (per-agent ablation — extracts intermediate phase scores for ablation arms). EFFORT: Sprint 7 ~2-3 days (vs DEC-042 ~1-2d): (a) AgentGateConfig dataclass with PM-native fields ~0.5d; (b) LangGraph state extraction for Risk debate + Research Manager confidence ~1d; (c) DEC-216 A/B orchestrator integration with revised arm definitions ~0.5d; (d) Risk continuous-score test infrastructure for owner directive #3 ~0.5d. TEST SIGNALS: (i) AgentGateConfig dataclass typed (confidence 0.0-1.0 invariant; Risk threshold 0.5 default; tier thresholds 0.5/0.65/0.8 default); (ii) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.85) + Research Manager(align long, conf=0.7) + Risk(conf=0.6) → HIGH-tier entry (5% size); (iii) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.85) + Risk(conf=0.4) → Risk veto → REJECT regardless of PM confidence; (iv) Synthetic PM(BUY, conf=0.7) + Research Manager(contested, conf=0.4) → align check fails → REJECT; (v) Synthetic PM(HOLD) → REJECT (decision direction); (vi) DEC-216 A/B orchestrator passes config per arm: full-with-veto = default; no-Risk = veto disabled; no-Bull-Bear-align = alignment disabled; (vii) Continuous-Risk A/B arm vs binary-veto arm produces measurable Sharpe delta documenting which is empirically better (REVISIT_AFTER_BACKTEST per owner directive #3 turn 121 carried forward); (viii) LangGraph state extraction unit tests verify Risk debate confidence + Research Manager confidence reachable from PM decision output. Pass 53 turn (this) owner-approved RESOLVED-DECIDED (Q1 = (c) expanding window). SUPERSEDES DEC-109 (rolling 5y train/1y OOS × 6 folds). Configuration: with current date May 2026 and 5y backward = May 2021, schedule is: Fold 1 train 2021-05 to 2023-05 (2yr); OOS 2023-05 to 2023-11 (6mo). Fold 2 train 2021-05 to 2023-11 (2.5yr expanded); OOS 2023-11 to 2024-05 (6mo). Fold 3 train 2021-05 to 2024-05 (3yr expanded); OOS 2024-05 to 2024-11 (6mo). Fold 4 train 2021-05 to 2024-11 (3.5yr expanded); OOS 2024-11 to 2025-05 (6mo). Fold 5 train 2021-05 to 2025-05 (4yr expanded); OOS 2025-05 to 2025-11 (6mo). Total 5 folds × 6mo OOS = 2.5yr OOS coverage; expanding train uses ALL available history per fold; consistent with academic walk-forward methodology under data-constrained conditions. Tradeoffs vs DEC-109: smaller OOS sample (6mo vs 12mo) → wider CIs in A/B comparison; less regime variation in earliest train (2021 was post-COVID bull); more frequent retraining captures regime evolution. | **DECISION-469** | Adopt Benjamini-Hochberg FDR (q=0.10) replacing Bonferroni multi-testing correction. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION (Cluster 1 Statistical Methodology); formally added to AUDIT_INDEX master table Pass 53 turn (this) — fixes phantom-DEC-label documentation gap. RATIONALE: Bonferroni at α=0.05 / 254,016 cube cells × 60 strategies × 6 regimes = α=3.3e-9 per test, infeasible to detect any signal. BH FDR controls expected proportion of false rejections among rejected nulls; q=0.10 means at most 10% of "PASS" cells are false positives. Less conservative than Bonferroni but appropriate for exploratory testing at scale. Resolves GAP 60 (CRITICAL — multiple-testing math doesn't reconcile). EFFORT: ~0.5d implementation in Sprint 7 statistical methodology. JOINT: DEC-470 (hierarchical structure), DEC-471 (cube reduction), DEC-110 (PSR), DEC-426 (5-Gate filter Gate 2). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic dataset with known true-positive rate produces FDR ≤ 0.10; comparison vs Bonferroni shows higher detection power. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. [was: PROPOSED] -- Batch 64 (2026-05-11 owner directive: Unblock all & resolve all) | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Sprint 7 / Statistical Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |
| **DECISION-470** | Hierarchical 3-level FDR application (per-strategy / per-cell / per-regime). Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). RATIONALE: Single-level FDR across ALL cube cells loses statistical power when most cells are uninteresting. Hierarchical: Level 1 (per-strategy: 119 strategies) controls family-wise error at strategy roster level; Level 2 (per-cell within passing strategy) controls error within active strategies; Level 3 (per-regime within passing cell) controls regime-conditional inference. Each level applies BH FDR at q=0.10. Resolves GAP 60 cascade with structure. EFFORT: ~1d implementation; test infrastructure ~0.5d. JOINT: DEC-469 (BH FDR foundation), DEC-471 (cube reduction reduces hierarchy depth), DEC-426 (5-Gate filter Gate 2), DEC-487 (Phase 1A-α cube verdict consumes). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic 3-level dataset preserves expected per-level FDR; cells failing Level 1 do not advance to Level 2/3. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. [was: PROPOSED] -- Batch 64 (2026-05-11 owner directive: Unblock all & resolve all) | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Sprint 7 / Statistical Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |
| **DECISION-471** | Cube dimensionality reduction to 8 core dimensions. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). RATIONALE: Original DEC-422 17+ dimensions × ~20 values/dim = 254,016+ cells; mean trades/cell = 0.024 (impossible to populate). Reduce to 8 core: strategy_id × regime × sector × cap_band × vol_band × hold_band × tier (now T1a/T1b/T1c/T2/T3 per DEC-483) × smart_money_signal. Eliminated dims (days_to_earnings/days_to_FOMC/news_topic/etc.) become trade-level metadata for filtering not as cube coordinates. Cell count: ~119 strategies × 4 regimes × 11 sectors × 3 caps × 3 vols × 3 holds × 5 tiers × 2 SM = 1,178,820 → after empirical pruning ~30-50k populated cells; mean trades/cell ~0.5-2 (testable with FDR). Resolves GAP 62 (CRITICAL — cube cell sparsity / compute cost unestimated). EFFORT: ~1d cube schema refactor; ~0.5d trade-level metadata logger. JOINT: DEC-422 (SUPERSEDES), DEC-426 (5-Gate filter), DEC-487 (Phase 1A-α cube populator), DEC-100 (17+ dim list deprecated). TEST SIGNALS: cube cell count target met; mean trades/cell ≥ 0.5 on representative sample. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. | **DECISION-472** | Eliminate paired A/B design; switch to independent arms with shared opportunity set + block bootstrap CIs. Pass 52 turn 133 PROPOSED via CRITICAL_GAPS_RESOLUTION; formally added to AUDIT_INDEX Pass 53 turn (this). RATIONALE: Paired A/B design (same trade in arm A vs arm B) is statistically invalid when arms execute different trades (which they do — arm B with agent overlay rejects some arm A trades, takes different ones). DEC-472 mandates independent arms operating on shared opportunity set (same universe + signals); paired test invalid → use block bootstrap (10-day blocks to preserve serial correlation) on Sharpe difference / win-rate difference between arms. Confidence intervals via percentile method. Resolves GAP 92 (CRITICAL — trade set pairing statistically invalid). EFFORT: ~1.5d block bootstrap implementation; ~0.5d arm independence verification. JOINT: DEC-205-216 (A/B operational — adapted), DEC-211 (per-agent ablation), DEC-242 (distribution analysis), DEC-487 (Phase 1A-α 1-arm baseline), DEC-488 (Phase 1A-β scale-validation). TEST SIGNALS: synthetic paired vs independent comparison shows different CIs; block bootstrap CIs cover known true difference at 95% level. AWAITS OWNER APPROVAL. [was: PROPOSED] -- Batch 64 (2026-05-11 owner directive: Unblock all & resolve all) | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Sprint 7 / A/B Methodology | Pass 52 | 53 |

| **DECISION-503** | **Comprehensive test pyramid before every code push (HARD RULE).** Pass 53 owner directive 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED. RULE: every code push (new feature / bug fix / refactor / schema change / data-source migration) must execute and pass ALL applicable test types: (1) Unit — individual function correctness with mocked dependencies; (2) Smoke — basic happy-path verification on real data ≤30s; (3) Integration — module-to-module data flow (e.g., fetcher → cache → signals → screener); (4) System — end-to-end workflow (full prefetch → universe load → backtest → report); (5) Functional — feature behavior matches spec; (6) Regression — full `backtest/tests/test_unit.py` + `backtest/tests/test_integration.py` (36/36 must pass); (7) Data integrity — schema validation, PIT semantics, completeness gates; (8) Performance / load — for prefetch + heavy-data code (rate-limit handling, memory bounds, wall-time budgets); (9) Acceptance — owner-defined pass criteria for the specific change. Partial coverage ("just unit tests" or "just smoke + integration") is non-compliant. If a test type doesn't apply (e.g., performance for a typo fix), explicitly mark N/A with reason. RATIONALE: silent-gap finding (BUG-271/272/273 — smart_money.py has been silently broken for 3 of 4 Quiver endpoints) went undetected because tests focused on `congresstrading` (which works) and skipped `insidertrading` + `institutionalholdings` + `analystestimates`. Comprehensive coverage would have caught this. Owner directive 2026-05-05: "Just dont do a few limited tests." TRIGGER: ANY code push — including doc-only repos that touch executable scripts. APPLY: codified CHECKLIST.md item #69 + CLAUDE.md HARD RULE Pass 53 (this turn). FIRST APPLICATION: smart_money silent-gap fix (BUG-271/272/273) next turn — full test pyramid required. JOINT: DEC-097 (90% test coverage minimum), DEC-098 (hot-path 100% coverage), CHECKLIST #69 (this DEC codifies), L145 (silent-gap lesson). | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Cross-cutting / process | Pass 53 | 53 |
| **DECISION-508** | **Smartmoneyconcepts fork extensive testing protocol (15-category plan + 3-phase A/B/C gate).** Pass 53 owner-approved Q1+Q2+Q3 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED. RULE: Any external library forked under DEC-045 (or future fork-first DECs) must complete the 15-category test plan + 3-phase gate before strategies consume its signals. CHECKLIST #71 codifies the rule. Q1 approve protocol; Q2 start Phase A testing NOW (not wait Sprint 8); Q3 owner does manual Dashboard 2 validation review. RATIONALE: ICT/SMC signals are pattern-based with subjective ground truth — 4 risks: (1) lookahead bias (library may compute swing high using future bars); (2) pattern-matching noise (algorithms find FVGs in random data); (3) subjective ground truth (no canonical right answer; need pinned-version reproducibility); (4) performance scale (1937 tickers × 1000 days × multiple primitives). FIFTEEN TEST CATEGORIES across 4 tiers: Tier 1 (correctness; must-pass before merge): unit tests on synthetic OHLCV (~50-100 tests), canonical pattern fixtures, **PIT correctness regression CRITICAL** (freezegun: signal at bar D unchanged when computed at D+50 per DEC-261 N+1 lag), edge case handling, library version pin + reproducibility. Tier 2 (integration; must-pass before strategy enable): cache pipeline integration, cross-primitive composition, survivorship + corporate actions, performance/load. Tier 3 (empirical validation; must-pass before backtest): statistical sanity (signal frequency bounds), adversarial random-walk test (library on Brownian motion ~ baseline rate not pattern-matching), cross-validation against known source (TradingView ICT script), signal lookahead detection (>65% win rate flag per DEC-084). Tier 4 (visual + manual): Dashboard 2 (DEC-200; existing Pass 52 turn 79 5-section spec), owner manual spot-check ~20-30 signals. THREE-PHASE GATE: Phase A (PRE-MERGE; library in vendored/, not main): all Tier 1+2+3 tests pass; ≥90% line coverage; library NOT imported outside test files; OWNER REVIEW + APPROVAL → Phase B. Phase B (CANARY; library imported, strategies disabled): signals computed for full universe → data_prefetch/ict_smc/{ticker}.parquet but strategies don't consume; Dashboard 2 launched; owner manual validates 20-50 signals; statistical sanity report; PIT regression on full universe; OWNER REVIEW + APPROVAL → Phase C. Phase C (PRODUCTION; strategies enabled): ICT/SMC Layer 2 strategies (per STRATEGY_REGISTER) consume signals; backtest run; A/B vs baseline comparison; DEC-084 red-flag check (>65% win rate = lookahead suspect); walk-forward validation per DEC-505 4 OOS folds; integration accepted. EFFORT: ~15-23d engineering + variable owner review (Tier 1 ~5-7d / Tier 2 ~2-3d / Tier 3 ~2d / Dashboard 2 ~3-4d / canary + owner review variable / production backtest ~1-2d). TIMING per Q2: Phase A starts NOW (Pass 53 Sprint 0A); not waiting Sprint 8. Q3: owner does manual review (no paid reviewer delegation). JOINT: DEC-045 (fork-first parent), DEC-200 (Dashboard 2), DEC-261 (PIT N+1 lag), DEC-084 (>65% win rate red flag), DEC-503 (test pyramid; Tier 1-3 USE pyramid layers), DEC-507 (wiring matrix; complementary), L147 (external library fork integration risk lesson; this DEC is first instantiation), CHECKLIST #71 (codifies this protocol). FIRST APPLICATION: smartmoneyconcepts library Phase A testing kicks off Pass 53 Sprint 0A as Batch 15. PATTERN REUSABLE: future forks (TradingAgents v0.2.4, future libraries) follow same 15-category + 3-phase protocol. | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Cross-cutting / process | Pass 53 | 53 |

BOUNDARY CONDITIONS for ceiling suspension: (1) Phase 1A empirical findings invalidate codified DEC; (2) Phase 1B-α 7-gate verdict produces zero PASS cells (Stage 2 has failed; Part 13.3 re-opens); (3) Live data source breaks (Polygon/Quiver/FRED/AAII/CNN F&G/CFTC/SEC EDGAR/Apewisdom/pytrends); (4) Owner explicit directive. RATIONALE: marginal utility of additional audit cycles approached zero per 7-review review-take saturation; CC-2 audit-cycle non-convergence + CC-3 decision-to-code ratio of ∞:0 + CC-4 planning-paralysis pattern surfaced in Review 7 adversarial. | **DECISION-504** | **T3-over-T1 multi-tier precedence rule (universe membership resolver).** Pass 53 owner directive 2026-05-05 RESOLVED-DECIDED ("If a ticker is in multiple tiers, rules of T3 should apply over T1"). PRECEDENCE ORDER (most-specific wins): T3 (momentum) > T2 (spinoff/IPO) > T1c (NDX-non-S&P) > T1a (S&P 500) > T1ETF. SCOPE owner Q1=Approve all (a)-(e): (a) Liquidity floor — T3=$5M ADV vs T1=$10M; T3 applies for dual T1+T3 tickers; (b) History minimum — T3=60d vs T1=250d; T3 applies; (c) Position sizing — tier-specific tier→size map; T3 sizing applies; (d) Strategy roster — T3-eligible strategies fire for resolved tier; (e) Refresh cadence — T3 monthly vs T1 quarterly (ops-level). RATIONALE: T3 captures NON-T1 momentum names; if a ticker graduates into T1 from T3 (rare; e.g., VST May 2024), the T3 momentum thesis is more recent + more specific. T3 rules should apply because T3 was the reason the ticker was screened. CANONICAL EXAMPLE: VST joined S&P 500 2024-05-08; T3 had it as momentum top-100 with added_date=2024-05-01, removed_date=2024-06-03. For as_of=2024-06-01, both T1a and T3 active → resolves to T3. For as_of=2024-07-01, T3 removed → resolves to T1a. IMPLEMENTATION: `backtest/data/universe.py` — `_TIER_PRECEDENCE` constant + `TIER_PARAMS` dict (per-tier liquidity/history/sizing/cadence) + `resolve_tier_precedence(ticker, as_of)` function returns most-specific tier name; `get_tier_params(ticker, as_of)` returns parameter dict for resolved tier. Master Dedup CSV gets new `resolved_tier` column (post-build). TEST SIGNALS (10 unit tests, all PASS): tier order = T3>T2>T1c>T1a>T1ETF; TIER_PARAMS complete with 5 required keys; T3 more permissive than T1a (ADV + history); VST 2024-06-01→T3, VST 2024-07-01→T1a; T2∩T3→T3; T1a-only→T1a; SPY→T1ETF; unknown ticker→None; get_tier_params returns mutation-safe copy. JOINT: DEC-040 (PointInTimeLoader contract), DEC-321/366 (T3 liquidity floor), DEC-103 (T2 thresholds), DEC-477 (T1a canonical), DEC-483 (T1c sub-tier), DEC-104 (T3 momentum methodology), CHECKLIST #69 (test pyramid first application). FIRST APPLICATION: this turn — DEC-504 implementation went through full test pyramid (10 new unit tests + integration + regression 79/79 + smoke + functional + acceptance). | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 53 / Sprint 0A | Pass 53 | 53 |

| **BUG-222** | S&P 500 constituents are CURRENT membership applied to all backtest dates - survivorship bias. RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED Batch 117 2026-05-12 (owner-approved option B 2026-05-12 - tier-specific PIT filter; option A flagged at the menu would have over-filtered T1 ETFs/T2/T3 out of the backtest universe). Added `get_t1a_master_set()` helper to `backtest/data/universe.py` returning the union of all tickers ever in the T1a master CSV (~614: 503 current + 111 historical removed-during-window). Engine's `_build_liquid_universe._annual_liquid` block now: (i) loads the T1a master set once at startup, (ii) loads the PIT S&P 500 set per year_start via `get_sp500_constituents_pit(ref_date)`, (iii) drops a ticker from the annual liquid set ONLY when it's in the T1a master AND NOT in the PIT membership at that year. T1 ETFs (e.g. SPY/QQQ), T2 (spinoffs/IPOs), T3 (momentum non-T1 names) bypass the PIT intersection entirely - they keep using OHLCV liquidity only. Graceful fallback: if the T1a master CSV is missing or unreadable, `get_t1a_master_set` returns an empty set, the engine skips the PIT intersection, and the pre-Batch-117 behavior is preserved. Net effect when T1a CSV is present: tickers added to S&P 500 mid-window become eligible only from their `added_date`; tickers removed become ineligible after their `removed_date`. Likely reduces survivorship inflation by 1-3pp ROI on long-window backtests of T1a-only strategies. 2 integration tests added (helper returns ~500+ tickers from real CSV; engine source-grep verifies the gate logic + t1a-master + t1a-pit-at-year filter). Full 13-tier pyramid: 712/712 mandatory T1-T10 green. | CRITICAL | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 48 |

Added `.github/workflows/refresh_aaii.yml` with cron `0 22 * * 4` (every Thursday 22:00 UTC, after AAII Thu-afternoon publication window) that installs pandas/openpyxl/xlrd, runs `python scripts/refresh_aaii_sentiment.py --cron`, and commits the new row with message `chore: AAII weekly sentiment refresh YYYY-MM-DD (BUG-236)`. Joint with BUG-235 (Batch 99 pub-lag fix): refresh runs AFTER Thu publication; loader gates by AAII_PUB_LAG_DAYS=1 so Wed-dated surveys become tradeable only Thu+. 2 integration tests added (workflow file exists + Thursday cron + script-path reference + --dry-run/--cron CLI contract). Full 13-tier pyramid: 687/687 mandatory T1-T10 green. | HIGH | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 48 |
| **BUG-237** | CNN F&G CSV interpolated between key readings - fabricated PIT signal. RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED Batch 102 2026-05-12 (engine wire of existing helper visibility). The 1630-row legacy `cnn_fear_greed.csv` mixes actual publications with interpolated bridge values; DEC-320 + DEC-391 (Pass 53 Batch 46) added the `days_since_publish` heuristic to `get_fear_and_greed(as_of)` in `backtest/data/sentiment.py` (run-length of identical scores = bridge-day count). Batch 102 wires this staleness signal into the engine: `OpenTrade.signals_at_entry["cnn_fg_days_since_publish"]` now captures the integer staleness count at entry time (`backtest/engine/backtest.py:1126-1136`). 0 = fresh publication day; high N = days since last actual reading change. Downstream metrics + agents can downweight trades entered against heavily-interpolated F&G readings. Active gating (skip trade when interpolation count > threshold) deferred per Phase 1A approved rules: surface visibility first, owner-tuned gating later. 2 integration tests added (source-grep BUG-237 comment + signals_at_entry tag; behavioral verifying days_since=5 flows + missing-key defaults to 0). Full 13-tier pyramid: 689/689 mandatory T1-T10 green. | HIGH | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 48 |
| **BUG-238** | Liquidity filter market-cap fail-open - missing data passes filter. RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED Batch 98 2026-05-12 (active engine fix, not status correction): `backtest/engine/backtest.py:_build_liquid_universe` previously used `if mkt_cap_m > 0 and mkt_cap_m < min: continue` which only rejected when market_cap data was present. Tickers with missing market_cap (delisted, recent IPO with stale ref row, Polygon reference gap) silently passed the gate. Fix at line 207-216: replaced fail-open pattern with `if _min_cap > 0 and mkt_cap_m < _min_cap: continue` - any ticker with mkt_cap_m < min (including 0/missing) is now dropped. Filter remains a no-op when LIQUIDITY config sets min_market_cap_m=0 (opt-out for tests / non-equity assets). 2 integration tests added (source-grep verifying BUG-238 comment + new fail-closed pattern; behavioral verifying mkt_cap=0 fails when min>0). Full 13-tier pyramid: 683/683 mandatory T1-T10 green. | HIGH | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 48 |

| **BUG-244** | close_trade circuit breaker exits skip MAE/MFE update on day of exit (passes 0.0). RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED via process_day_exits order-of-operations at `backtest/engine/exit_manager.py:509-520` (MAE/MFE updated using today's high/low) which precedes the circuit-breaker check at line 523. So when a CB exit fires at line 528+ and calls `close_trade`, the trade's `max_adverse_excursion` + `max_favourable_excursion` already include today's bar. `close_trade` reads these from the OpenTrade (`max_adverse_excursion=round(trade.max_adverse_excursion, 4)` at line 413) and persists them on the resulting ClosedTrade. The pass-0.0 symptom described in the original BUG would only occur if the order were reversed; current code is correct. Batch 121 2026-05-12 false-positive OPEN correction: 2 integration tests added (source-grep verifying MAE/MFE-update precedes CB-check ordering; behavioral verifying close_trade reads from OpenTrade not fresh-computed zeros). Full 13-tier pyramid: 716/716 mandatory T1-T10 green. | HIGH | RESOLVED-IMPLEMENTED | Pass 48 |
